## 1. The promise

**What a person sees after Build 1** (parts A, B, F and G; part V is a build of its own — §10, §19).
- With a window open that holds something to edit — an Input, Leave War or other window, the OIL list among them
  (D2403) — a pick of another squadron, the switch to or from the member view, and a bell line or a tap on a lit bell
  that would take him to another squadron do nothing but raise the app's grey "Blocked:" note: under the control
  pressed wherever the top bar shows the squadron picker, at the screen's foot on an upright phone and for any press
  inside the ☰ menu (D2590, D2627 A14–A16). The window stays live with his typing; the squadron and the view stay as they were.
- **A window that only shows things closes by itself at those same presses, and the press goes through, no note:**
  "Who's available", Changes (opened out or folded to its bar), Insights, a day opened on the SANS calendar, the Leave
  War's day panel, a Tracker event's details (D2596, D2597). **The Calendar window, the Tracker's marking window, its Down days and Failures, and a settings window
  with nothing changed in it close this way too (D2654 — E1, E2, E4); the Calendar refuses instead while one of its
  forms or a sheet of holidays read in is half done, and a settings window with a change refuses, the note naming it
  (§8.3).** ~~Each is still his to answer and closes this way only on his word.~~
- The same presses are refused while a change is not saved — the "Not saved" band with Retry (D2594), the note
  standing under the band — and while Admin → Users holds a change not saved: an opened row with anything changed in
  it, the Give access form, "+ New squadron", and "Add a person" once something is in it (D2595, D2598). "Add a
  person" is now one button beside Filter at the top of the page, its form opening in place with "Add person" and
  "Cancel" (D2599, D2628 C11). **Seen and taken as drawn (D2655 D1), the two buttons one pair of the same size (D2644); the button
  stands in the page's top row with the search and Filter — built there once, by step 1b — and the form opens under
  that row (D2652 N13 i — §18.3).**
- A press made while a save is still going waits for that save: it lands and the press goes on, or "Not saved" shows
  and the press is refused with the Retry note — never a wait without end (D2627 A9).
- **The same refusal meets every press that would bring his own squadron back from one he only views** — a bell line
  that opens one of his own pages, a tap on a lit bell, a card of the OIL list. **And nothing opens behind a refusal**
  — the window, the question or the passing note that press would have brought is run by the page's own door, and
  only once the page is there (§4.5). The "Welcome back" card shows only on his own squadron, so "Check my quals"
  never changes squadron (D2628 C4; D2627 A18).
- Unsaved Tracker chart edits are asked about at the switch to and from the member view, always, both ways, with the
  question a pick asks today (D2403; D2627 A20). A refusal always comes before that question, so he never discards
  chart edits only to be refused (D2627 A10).
- A pick ends Logic's and Quals' edit mode: the squadron picked opens as it normally does (D2627 A4).
- **No press on the Inputs pages, the bell or the OIL list changes a 142 input under a bar reading 149 any more.**
  **Two roads are left, both named to him:** a moved man's old input, answered where he is (his ruling, D2621), and
  **Undo or Redo of a 142 step while 149 is picked** — on 149 Undo still offers a Plan holder his 142 step and lands on
  149's Inputs page (item 3 below; Build 3, part E; Fable 7 of the third read). With another squadron picked, the
  Inputs page lists none
  of his own inputs of a squadron whose Inputs page he can still open — **the just-saved row pinned at the List's head
  among them (§5.1)** — Hunter's 142 duty stays on 142's page, off
  149's list and calendar, its "OIL?" button with it (D2621); a tap on the bell for his own OIL question, a holiday's
  bell line and a card of the OIL list each bring the input's squadron into view first (D2621, D2627 A19; security F1
  and F2); and the Input window keeps the input's own squadron. A man who has moved, and whose pages can never be his
  old squadron's, still sees his old inputs where he is, and answers them there (D2621).
- The squadron number at the top left dims and comes back twice, in about a second, whenever the squadron in view
  changes; never at sign-in, never at a refused pick (D2620, D2628 C10). **On a phone the two fades wait until the ☰ menu has closed — and
  wherever else the number is covered when the squadron changes — so both are seen (D2642, D2652 N8, narrowing D2620
  and D2628 C10 as to when the fade runs).**

**What stays exactly as today** (each pinned by a test written before the changed half — §7): a pick or a view switch
with nothing open; the Tracker's question at a pick, its three answers; menus and lists closing as they do; Traffic,
Jump to a date, a day's ⓘ and a document closing at a press on the top bar (D2627 A1); the Scheduler Board, which is
no window (D2627 A3); a pick of the squadron already in view; a bell line that changes neither the squadron nor the
page (an input since deleted, his own account's line); Logout (never refused — its questions are group B's, put to him
after this build: D2636); the "Not kept" band blocking nothing; and **every change he did not make** — his own move at
midnight, a right taken away, the Tracker failing part-way.

**What Build 1 leaves half-way, said plainly.**
1. **The greyed windows and their bands still exist** (`state/typing-hold.ts`, `ui/heldro.ts`, `state/store.ts
   publishBands`, `ui/InputWindow.tsx`, `leavewar/ui/Sheet.tsx`). After Build 1 no press of his reaches the pick's band
   (D2323) or the member view's band (D2389): those two become code no road leads to. The midnight and right-ended
   bands show exactly as today until Build 2 (part D) replaces them with the refresh card (D2405).
2. **The Tracker's hold and its member-view cover** (`store.ts trkMayHold`, `coverWords`; `tracker/TrackerPage.tsx`)
   stay in the code. Whether his own switch still reaches the cover is N10's (§4.4); the hold at his own move at
   midnight is unchanged.
3. **Undo still reaches another squadron's steps** (part E, Build 3 — on 149 Undo offers a 142 step today: P6 C-undo)
   — **so a 142 input can still be changed under a bar reading 149 by Undo or Redo until Build 3**; and
   Logout still draws the Input window over the Tracker's question (part C, Build 2). **All of group B waits for his
   answers after this build (D2636).** **An Undo of "+ New squadron" while that squadron is in view** (§4.1 row 21 — the
   page's B7) **is refused in Build 1 while a window is open or a change is not saved (his answer, D2654 E7 — step
   6b);** ~~or left as today until group B~~ **(not taken).**
4. **A window held after midnight, then his own pick: the pick goes through and gives it back, as today (his
   answer, D2654 E5 — the plan's N6).**

**Is that interim state safe to merge alone? Only on three conditions.**
1. **Row 21 has his answer — met: refused in Build 1 (D2654 E7).** An Undo of "+ New squadron" while that squadron is in view changes the squadron
   with a window open or a change not saved; he put the page's B7 off with group B (D2636) without being told it is a
   door of this guard, and the round-5 page now tells him and asks. Refused in Build 1 (default, step 6b), or left
   open on his word — never left open unsaid.
2. **No answer still open is built before he gives it** — NQ1–NQ3, N14, V-Q2, §17's readings 17–24 and §8.5's
   list (E1–E9, D5, D6, D8 and D10–D12 are answered: D2654, D2655): each row and step that waits on one says so (§8.4, §8.5,
   §10, §21).
3. **Every new look and word has been drawn and seen by him** (D1625) — the note's new words, the Add a person button
   and its Cancel, the fades (a short film) — and every `[WNS]` word has had its fresh Fable's read (D2441).

Security F2, which `[MSQ-NIGHT-153-OPEN]` has fixed before "merge live", is closed by this build on every road of the
Inputs pages, the bell and the OIL list (D2621, §5); the two places a man still changes another squadron's input from
where he is — a moved man's old input, his ruling, and Undo or Redo across a pick, Build 3's — are named in the
backlog item, the first in his words. With the three conditions, Build 1 only removes ways into a held
window and closes those faults; nothing a person can do today that is right stops working, and nothing new is held.
The cost of merging it alone is two bands' worth of unreachable code for the length of one build, and the 22-test
file that proves them by a pick is re-pointed, not deleted (§10). It must **not** be merged before its own FULL check
(§10).

## 2. Where the sizing file is wrong about today's code

| Sizing file says | What the code does |
|---|---|
| Three doors: the pick, the view switch, a bell line (§1, part B) | **Four families.** `state/store.ts landOnOwnPage` — run from `landOnOfferedPage` on every `notify()` — enters his own squadron (`enterSquadron(home, …, true)`) whenever any press opens a page a squadron he only views does not offer. It has no list of callers: a bell line, the bell's own taps, the OIL list's card, "Check my quals" (`ui/WelcomeBack.tsx`), and an Undo or Redo whose step lands on another page (`state/undo-wire.ts snapView`) all reach it. |
| "No one list covers the app… the shell's windows, the OIL list and the Tracker's each stand alone"; part A "each window registers as it opens and leaves as it closes", about twenty-five to register | **The frames already keep lists.** `ui/winorder.ts UP` holds every `FloatWin` that is up (joined by `forward(id)` in `ui/FloatWindow.tsx`'s own layout effect, left by `gone(id)` at unmount — ten ids) **and two more, `'avail'` and `'chg'`, which ALL AVAIL and Changes put there themselves** (`ui/availopen.ts`, `ui/changesopen.ts`); `leavewar/ui/Sheet.tsx OPEN` every Leave War sheet (23 uses of `<Sheet`), already read by `state/typing-hold.ts warWindowOpen()`; `ui/sheetfocus.ts sheets` every sheet that holds the keys (thirteen callers — the dialogs and the document viewer among them, so it is no list of question sheets: §3.1); `tracker/app/core.js endSession()` names every Tracker window it closes. About 45 open-states in 8 frames (§3.2), not 25 windows. **A list kept by a frame is a list of what is mounted or flagged, not of what is drawn — the red team's main correction to this row (§3.1).** |
| `ui/pops.ts` holds the schedule side's flags | True for 14 windows. Not there: ALL AVAIL, Changes and the OIL question put before a change (`state/view.ts AVAILWIN`, `CHGWIN`, `OILWAIT`); four movable windows whose open-state is a component's own (`inputsday`, `sansday`, `everywd`, `holiday`); the board's two dialogs (`ui/board.ts`, read from the page by `pops.ts windowOverSchedule`). |
| A change not saved — `ui/savestatus-source.ts readFailed()` | Right, but a door in `state/` may not import `ui/`. The same answer from the same saver is `storage/savewait.ts saveRefused()` (`main.tsx` hands one postman to both). |
| The view switch "asks nothing, the Tracker held under a cover" → ask at the view switch, both ways | The switch moves the Tracker only for a Plan holder or master admin with a squadron other than his home in view (`state/perms.ts pageUnit('own')` falls back to his home in the member view). For an admin at home it moves nothing. And no road reaches the switch **back** with unsaved edits on a Tracker that would move: a pick of a squadron he only views already asks (D2637). So the "switch back" wording has a path only where the Tracker would not move — answered: asked always, both ways (D2627 A20). |
| Part F: `ui/InputWindow.tsx`, `ui/OilDayList.tsx`, `ui/topstate.ts` | Also `state/belltap.ts enterForLine`; a third door (a holiday's bell line opening 142's OIL list over 149's View-only Sched — answered, D2627 A19: its squadron first); and the stopped builder's Catch A — `engine/inputs.ts inputsListed` lists every input naming him on whatever squadron's Inputs page he has open (`ui/InputsPage.tsx inputsPageRows`, `ui/InputsCal.tsx calRows`), where the row's own "OIL?" button answers its OIL question with no window at all (`reviseOil`) — answered, D2621: no longer listed there (§5.1). |
| "Ten test files pin" the held machinery | 37 test files press a pick or the view switch and 13 a bell line (§10 step 0). `ui/msq-pick-windows.test.tsx` alone has 22 tests that reach a held window by a pick the guard will now refuse. |
| Part B: `tracker/app/core.js` (the question's second wording) | Also `tracker/role.js trackerSwitchAsk`: the question's tail is built in core.js as `'switching to ' + to`, which cannot say "switching back to the admin view". |

## 3. Part A — one answer to "what is open"

### 3.1 The design chosen: one function over one table, each row reading the very predicate its window is drawn from

**Not a registry that each window joins and leaves.** A registry is a second copy of "is it open" beside what the
window is drawn from, kept true only by a call at every way out. Four of this app's frames do not unmount when they
close — the schedule's veiled dialogs are `hidden`, not removed (`ui/Modals.tsx`, `ui/SchedBoard.tsx CxDialog`); the
Tracker's windows are module state set to `null` in a dozen places (`core.js`); ALL AVAIL and Changes keep a mounted
placeholder; `pops.ts resetPopsForSession` clears flags with no component told. A join whose leave is missed on one of
those paths leaves **a guard that refuses with nothing open, with no way out but a reload** — the worst fault this
feature can have. And a window added later that never joins is refused nothing, silently.

**One function that asks what each window is drawn from** has no second copy to go stale. Its own fault is the mirror
one — a new window nobody adds to the table — and that one can be caught by tests that read the source (§3.4), which a
missed `leave()` cannot.

**"The flag" is not "the drawing", in six places the red team found (§15, findings 2, 4, 15, 17; §16, finding 6), and
each is the same trap — the guard counts something while nothing in sight can close it:**

| A flag or list that says "open" while nothing can be closed | Read in the code |
|---|---|
| Duty templates, Flying waves, Day templates, Plans | each is drawn only while he may edit the schedule: `DutyTplModal.tsx` `open = TPLEDIT && !(SESSION && !canEditSched())`; the same in `WaveTplModal.tsx` and `DayTplModal.tsx`; `DraftsModal.tsx` `up` also needs `CURPAGE === 'editsched'`. The four flags are cleared at a sign-out only (`pops.ts POPS_RESET`). A Plan lowered to View under an open dialog hides it and leaves the flag set |
| Changes | `ChangesWindow.tsx` `chgUp = !!w && isMember()` |
| The question sheets' list, `sheetfocus.ts sheets` (`topSheet()`) | thirteen callers of `useSheetFocus`, not six — the Input dialog, Insights, the four template dialogs, the board's two, and **the document viewer** (`DocViewer.tsx` l.121), a bar-free window that does not count. A sheet leaves the list in an effect's clean-up, after React has redrawn — so at a one-press door (the badge, the bell) a viewer the same press has just closed is still in it |
| The Tracker's module state | `tracker/App.jsx` draws no window while `core.bootError` (the "Couldn't finish upgrading your Tracker data" card, which has no button — pressed, P4 2f) or `!core.ready` ("Loading…"), and only `DlgModal` while there is no course; and nothing under the member-view cover can be reached (`TrackerPage.tsx` `locked`) |
| A window on a page that is not on screen | the Leave War and the Tracker stay mounted when another tab shows (`Shell.tsx`, `.page.doze`), their windows with them; **and a page the squadron in view does not offer has no tab to go back by** — §3.3 |
| A Tracker window inside its Info panel, on a screen 1050 wide or less showing the Flow chart | `tracker/tracker.css`: `@media(max-width:1050px){ … .tr-root.tab-flow .sidecol{display:none} }`; `components/SidePanel.jsx` draws the lull calendar, the lull copy, the fail log and Down days inside that column; `App.jsx` starts on `useState('flow')`. Opened wide, the screen then narrowed or turned: the window's state is set and nothing of it is drawn. He is on the Tracker already, so "…on the Tracker" tells him nothing — **N12** |

**The rule, for every row.** `open()` is the very predicate its component draws from: **one exported function, which
the component itself calls** for its early return or its `hidden` — never a raw flag where the two can differ. **It
lives in a plain module, never in the component's own file:** here a file that draws a component exports only
components (React Doctor's only-export-components, D1534 — `ui/winorder.ts`, `ui/topstate.ts` and
`leavewar/ui/daypanelopen.ts` were split out for it), and a new finding fails the pull request's own check (D1539).
Every predicate of §3.2 (b), (c) and (e) is built from module state alone (`TPLEDIT && !(SESSION && !canEditSched())`,
`CXT != null`, `!!AVAILWIN`, `!!CHGWIN && isMember()` …), so they go together in one new file, **`ui/windrawn.ts`**,
which each component and `ui/guard.ts` import. R1 checks the wiring by reading the source; A10 proves, by driving the
app, that a row that says "open" has its box on the page.

So:

- **`src/ui/guard.ts` (new).** `GUARD_ROWS`: one row per open-state — `{ id, name, frame, where, open(), sel, counts,
  close?, why? }`. `open()` is the predicate above. `sel` is the selector of the window's own box, **read by tests
  only** (A10, the browser tests) — production never decides by the page's nodes. `counts` is `'window' | 'row' |
  'unsaved' | 'closes' | 'no'`; a `'no'` row carries its reason in `why` and is in the table **so that the list is
  complete and a reader can see it was considered**. **A `'closes'` row is a window that only shows things (D2596,
  D2597): it never refuses, and it carries `close()` — its component's own way of closing it** (`closeAvailWin`,
  `closeChanges`, `setInsights(false)`, `closeDayPanel`; a closer the component hands over where its state is its own —
  `useGuardCloses`, below). `where` is the page the thing is drawn on (`'any'` for one drawn over every page).
  `whatIsOpen(skip?): { reason, row } | null` walks the rows in `GUARD_ORDER` and answers the first that is open and
  counts `'window'`, `'row'` or `'unsaved'`; `skip` names row ids a door leaves out — **every window that can be
  skipped has a row of its own** (§3.2 (a)), so a door leaves out exactly the window it was pressed in and still meets
  any other (two uses, both in §4.1: the held Input window's own chip, and a card pressed inside the OIL list).
  **`closeShown()` closes every `'closes'` row that is open — and ends the two edit modes (§18.1) — called only inside
  the final entry, once nothing can still stop the change:
  after the guard has passed, after a save waited for has landed, after the Tracker's question is answered Save or
  Discard and the guard has passed again — at a pick, at the view switch, and inside `landOnOwnPage` for the page door
  and `ownSquadronFirst` (§4.2 step 6; Astra 5 and Fable 10 of the third read: the earlier draft closed them at the
  page door before the landing's question). A refusal, a Stay, and a change he did not make
  close nothing.**
  **A row may carry `dirty?: () => boolean` beside a `close`** — E1's shape (default): it refuses only while
  `dirty()`, with its own lead, and is closed by `closeShown()` otherwise. The settings windows (`inputsset`,
  `sansset`: anything changed since it opened) and the Calendar's two forms take it; **built only on his answer to
  E1** — on its other answer each stays a plain `window` row and nothing else moves. `ui/` may import everything it must read: `ui/pops.ts`, `ui/winorder.ts`, `ui/windrawn.ts`,
  `state/view.ts`, `state/typing-hold.ts`, `tracker/role.js`, `storage/savewait.ts`.
- **`src/state/guard.ts` (new, no imports).** The doors live in `state/` and may not import `ui/`, so this file is the
  seam, in the repo's own pattern (`setWarWindowsProbe`, `setSessionWorldHook`, `onBeforeTrackerSwitch`):
  `setGuardAsk(fn)`, `setGuardSay(fn)`; `refusalAt(door, to, opts?)` — asks, says nothing; `refuse(door, to, opts?):
  boolean` — asks, and where refused says the note; `opts` is `{ from?: 'bar' | 'drawer' | 'board'; skip?: string[];
  line?: string }` — where the control pressed stands, the rows left out, the bell line pressed — **and one door's
  `opts` travel with its press the whole way: to `enterForLine`, to the page door, to the landing's second ask and to
  the checker** (§4.5, §3.4); `setEntryChecker(fn)` (§3.4, tests only). With nothing installed nothing is ever open
  and nothing refuses.
  **And for the windows that close by themselves:** `setGuardClose(fn)` / `closeShown()` — the door's one call once
  its change is going through (§4.2); with nothing installed it does nothing.
- **Who installs it.** `ui/guard.ts` installs itself as it loads, and it is imported by `ui/guardnote.ts`, which every
  door's own control imports (`SquadronMenu.tsx`, `Drawer.tsx`, `Shell.tsx`, `topstate.ts`) — so a build that can press
  a door has the guard. `test-support/msq-world.ts` and the two test set-ups import it too (§3.4): it now pulls in
  plain modules only, no component.
- **Typing that lives inside a component** (Admin's rows and forms — no module flag exists) reports through one small
  hook in `ui/guard.ts`, `useGuardWork(id, on)`: an effect that counts the id up while `on` and down in its clean-up, so
  React's own unmount is the leave. `id` is typed to the table's row ids — a report for a row that is not in the table
  does not compile. **Seven Admin states report, each its own** (D2595 as widened by D2598 — §3.2 (h)):
  `UsersPanel.tsx PersonEditor`, `ArchivedEditor`, `Waiting` (the Give access form) and the "Add a person" form, whose
  state is `UsersPanel`'s own (`name`, `np`, `role`, `postIn`, `home`); `SquadronsPanel.tsx NewSquadron`, `MoveEditor`,
  `PlaceEditor`. `on` is "anything differs from how it opened" in each.
  **An eighth report is a window, not typing:** the OIL question raised from a row of the Inputs list, whose state is
  the page's own (§3.2 (e)). **Two more reports, found by the third read, each typed work no frame holds:**
  `holimport` — a holiday sheet read in and not yet added (`ui/HolidaysPanel.tsx` `pv`, the panel's own state inside
  the Calendar: Astra 3; it counts on every answer to E1 but "always close"); and `qualsnew` — words typed in Quals'
  "Add qualification" box (`ui/QualsPage.tsx` `newQual`, added only by Add or Enter: Opus F-c — §18.1, a
  **(reading)**). The holiday sheet is the worked case of the limit below. The Admin page unmounts when its tab is left (pressed, P2 1g: the row is closed and its
  words gone on return), so no count outlives it. **A sister hook, `useGuardCloses(id, on, close)`,** registers the
  closer of a `'closes'` window whose state is its component's — the SANS day (`ui/SansCal.tsx`'s `dayIso`) — for
  exactly as long as the component is mounted with it open. **Its known limit, said and not tested for:** a form added
  later whose typing lives only in its own component, in no frame, is seen by no scan — the build's records say so in
  `feature-impact.md` (§10 step 9).
- **The Tracker's windows** are known only inside `core.js`, which Raptor must not import (it would pull the chart
  engine into the first download — `tracker/role.js`'s head). So `core.js` gains one **read-only** table, `TRK_WINDOWS`
  — `{ id, open: () => !!pop, counts, panel? }` for each window state (`panel: 'info'` for the four the Info panel
  draws — N12) — and **`endSession()` is left exactly as it is** (it does more than null the states — `dlgCancelAll()`
  answers every waiting question, the gestures and the Undo lists end). R4 ties the two lists by reading the source.
  `tracker/role.js` gains `onTrackerWindows(f)` / `trackerWindowsOpen(): { id, behind: 'info' | null }[]`; `core.js`
  registers, beside `onBeforeTrackerSwitch`, a function that answers **none unless the Tracker is drawn** — `ready &&
  !bootError`, and with no course only `dlg` (the three gates of `App.jsx`) — and marks `behind: 'info'` on a panel
  window while the phone layout shows the Flow chart (`App.jsx` hands its `tab` to core beside `setTabSink`; the width
  is the stylesheet's own `(max-width:1050px)`). **Two rows in `ui/guard.ts`:** `trk`, `where: 'tracker'`, `open() =
  !trackerCover() && trackerWindowsOpen().length > 0` (`state/typing-hold.ts trackerCover` — the page's own cover;
  what it does with a window `behind` is N12's); and `trkask`, `where: 'any'`, `open() = trackerAsking()` — the
  unsaved-edits question while `core.switchAsking && dlg`: it is drawn over whatever page he is on, the cover stepped
  aside for it (`TrackerPage.tsx asking`, `.trk-askonly`), so the `trk` row's page and cover gates would call it "not
  open" while it is on screen; the `trk` row leaves `dlg` out while it stands. The question's own door asks the guard
  again only after the answer, when `dlg` is gone (`core.js ask` clears `switchAsking` in its `finally`). Never
  loaded: nothing open.
  **And one closer:** `role.js` gains `onTrackerCloseShown(f)` / `trackerCloseShown()`; `core.js` registers a function
  that sets `infoId` to null and redraws — the event's details, the Tracker's one `'closes'` window he has ruled
  (D2597); its row in `ui/guard.ts` is `trkinfo`, `counts: 'closes'`, `close: trackerCloseShown`. **The same closer
  clears `pop` on E2's default and `downLog` and `failLog` on E4's** — one line each, added only on his answer.
  `TRK_WINDOWS` itself stays read-only.

### 3.2 The windows, found in the code

**The `counts` column is what is built.** A cell reads `window`, `row` or `unsaved` (it refuses), **`closes`** (it only
shows things: it closes by itself at the press and the press goes through — D2596, D2597), or `no` (a list, a menu, a
mode, a pop-up that already closes). Where a ruling or his answer names the row, the cell cites it. **Two kinds of
cell are not his answer, and say so:** a cell marked **E1, E2, E4 or E5 (N13, N7, N12, N6)** is a question still open
— the row is shaped for either answer, its **default** the round-5 page's recommendation, and not built until he
gives one; and **a cell marked (reading)** is a window the questions
page never showed him singly — the Tracker's Show All and its side pop-ups, the Leave War's sheets that only show
things, the board's two dialogs — held as `window` on D2403's "or other window" and D2597's own words ("none is added
on the agent's reading"): the safe side, since a refusal loses nothing and a wrong close might. The host puts the list
to him (§17). "Shaded" = the frame's own veil covers the bar, so no mouse reaches a door (the keys may — the browser
tests, §4.7).

**(a) The shell's movable windows — `ui/FloatWindow.tsx FloatWin`. One row per id, made from one map in `ui/guard.ts`,
`FLOAT_WHERE` (id → the page it is drawn on): `open() = winUp(id)` (a new read-only export of `ui/winorder.ts`:
`UP.includes(id)`). Not shaded: the page behind works (D641).** A row each, not one for the frame: a door's `skip`, a
row's `where` and the note's "where" are all about one window, and one row answering "is any up" could neither leave
out the OIL list for its own card nor still refuse for a second window beside it. And `UP` holds `'avail'` and `'chg'`
too (§2): they are not in the map — ALL AVAIL and Changes have their own rows in (b), from their own predicates, so
that they close by themselves (D2596) is those two rows and nothing here. A `FloatWin` joins `UP` in its own layout
effect and leaves at unmount, so for this frame mounted is drawn — except `lwday`, which sits on a page that stays mounted out of sight (§3.3). `inputedit`, `days`, `sansset`, `inputsset` and `oilday` are
mounted at the app's top level (`ui/App.tsx`) and stand over whatever page is shown: 142's OIL list stays open and live
over 149's View-only Sched after a pick today, and its Save writes 142's input under a bar reading 149 (pressed, P2 4).

| id | What | File · how it opens · how it closes | Its own flag | counts |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputedit` | The Input window on the Inputs page | `ui/InputWindow.tsx`; `setInpEdit(r)`; Cancel / Close, Add / Save, ✕ and Escape (which ask when something is typed, D1622) | `ui/pops.ts INPEDIT` | window |
| `inputsday` | A day opened on the Inputs calendar | `ui/InputsCal.tsx renderPop`; a day tapped; ✕, Escape. Today it stays open across a pick and shows 149's day, a half-typed "+ Note" landing on 142's (pressed, P4 4) | the calendar's own state | window |
| `sansday` | A SANS day | `ui/SansDay.tsx`, opened from `ui/SansCal.tsx` (`dayIso`, the calendar's own state); ✕, Escape. Today a pick leaves it open (from the code: nothing clears `dayIso` at an entry — the chat-176 presser confirms) | the SANS calendar's own state | **closes** (D2597) — its closer handed over by `useGuardCloses('sansday', !!dayIso, …)` |
| `inputsset` / `sansset` | Inputs / SANS calendar settings | `ui/InputsSettings.tsx`, `ui/SansSettings.tsx`; the gear or Logic's row; ✕, Escape, a page change (`VIEW_RESET 'page'`). **The Calendar opens beside it and it stays open** (`InputsSettings.tsx openDays`: `openBeside('days', 'inputsset')` — read on this branch; on a phone it lies under the Calendar) | `pops.ts INPSET`, `SANSSET` | window — **E1's second half (default):** `dirty` = anything changed since it opened; unchanged, it closes with the pick; changed, it refuses under its own lead (§4.6). Other answer: always `window`, as round 2 had it |
| `days` | Calendar | `ui/DaysWindow.tsx`; ✕, Escape. Each day pressed is saved at once; **it also holds what waits for a Save — the two forms below and a holiday sheet read in (the `holimport` report, §3.1)** | `pops.ts DAYSWIN` | **E1 (N13) — default: `closes`** with its own setter in `pops.ts`, while none of its forms or its sheet holds something not saved (each of those refuses by its own row). Other answers: `window` whenever open; or `closes` always (a typed holiday name lost, as today) |
| `everywd` / `holiday` | Every Monday · Add / Change a holiday | `ui/EveryWeekday.tsx`, `ui/HolidaysPanel.tsx`; opened from Calendar; ✕, Escape, their own Save | their opener's own state | window — **on E1's default each takes `dirty`:** a holiday with anything typed or changed; Every Monday once something in it is pressed, not as it opens with one choice lit (**(reading)** — the page's E9m). A new holiday no leave period covers, which the app keeps and saves later by itself: counted, its lead its own — read from the code, pressed before it is built (the page's E1) |
| `oilday` | The OIL list | `ui/OilDayList.tsx`; `setOilList(…)` — by itself when a holiday is saved, from a holiday's bell line, a sign-off box, a version loaded back; Save, Later, Close, ✕, Escape | `pops.ts OILLIST` | window (D2403 names it) |
| `lwday` | The Leave War's day panel | `leavewar/ui/DayPanel.tsx`; `openDayPanel(iso)`; ✕, Escape, `closeDayPanel()` | `leavewar/ui/daypanelopen.ts OPEN` | **closes** (D2597) — `close: closeDayPanel`. It sits on a page that stays mounted out of sight; closing it there is the same call |
| — | The `?timing` readout (pull request 840) | a `FloatWin` with `bystander`, which never calls `forward(id)` | — | **no** — it never joins `UP`; §9 |

**(b) The two older movable windows — `ui/floatwin.ts useFloatWin` directly; flags in `state/view.ts`.** Each keeps a
mounted, hidden placeholder, so the row reads the component's own predicate (`AvailWindow.tsx isOpen`,
`ChangesWindow.tsx chgUp` — today locals of the component's body; each becomes a function of `ui/windrawn.ts`). Today
both close without a word at a pick and at the view switch, in the one press (pressed, P1 2 and 6).

| What | File · closes | Flag | counts |

| ALL AVAIL ("Who's available") | `ui/AvailWindow.tsx`; ✕, Escape, a page or week change (`view.setPage`, `VIEW_RESET 'week'`) | `AVAILWIN` | **closes** (D2596) — as today; `close: closeAvailWin` (`ui/availopen.ts`) |
| Changes, opened out — and, on a phone only, folded to its bar at the foot (a desktop draws no "Hide", so it has no folded form: pressed, P1 2) | `ui/ChangesWindow.tsx`; the same | `CHGWIN` | **closes** (D2596), opened out and folded alike — `close: closeChanges` (`ui/changesopen.ts`) |

**(c) The schedule's veiled dialogs — `.airpop` / `.modal`, flags in `ui/pops.ts`. Shaded.** Each row reads the

Mounted at the top level, so it is never set and out of sight (Opus's check) | `INPEDIT` · the component's `open` | window |
| Insights | `ui/Modals.tsx InsightsModal` | `INSIGHTS` · the predicate its `useSheetFocus` is given | **closes** (D2597) — `close: () => setInsights(false)`. Shaded, so only a door reached by the keys meets it |

Their shade covers every door, the board's own bell and ✓ Done included (pressed, P1 3) | `ui/board.ts CXT`, `SORTALL` · the `open` each hands to `useSheetFocus` — `CXT != null`, `SORTALL != null` (l.880, l.974) — not the page query `pops.ts windowOverSchedule` uses, which a hidden board could still match (not traced: A10 answers it) | window (told on the page's A3: with either up "there is nothing to press and nothing to decide") |

**(d) The four veiled windows that leave the bar free — `ui/barfree.ts useBarFree` (D1645).** Any press on the bar other
than Undo or Redo closes them first (a `click` listener at the document's capture, keyboard clicks included), so by the
time a door's own handler runs their flag is already off. Pressed, P1 1: each closes without a word and the pick or the
switch goes through in the same press; Traffic's one box saves as it is typed, so nothing in it is lost.

| What | File | Flag | counts |
|---|---|---|---|
| Traffic · Day details ⓘ · Jump to a date · the document viewer | `ui/Modals.tsx AirPop`, `DayPop`; `ui/WeekCal.tsx`; `ui/DocViewer.tsx` | `AIRKEY`, `DAYPOP`, `WEEKCAL`, `DOCVIEW` | **no** — they close themselves at the press, as today (D2627 A1) |

The document viewer over an Input window with typing: on a desktop the bar stays free over both and today the pick goes
through, the Input window left greyed with its band scrolled out of sight; on a phone the viewer covers every door
(pressed, P1 1). After Build 1 the viewer closes at the press and the Input window under it refuses (B4).

**(e) The question sheets.** No row reads the sheets' own list, `topSheet()` — it holds what does not count (the table
of §3.1). In its place:
- the OIL question, the upchit summary, the medical clash and "no document" raised inside the Input window
  (`OilConfirm.tsx`, `UpchitConfirm.tsx`, `MedClashConfirm.tsx`, `DocConfirm.tsx`) stand over a window already counted
  — no row of their own; nor have the ask cards (`ui/AskCard.tsx`) of the Input window, of a calendar day's window and
  of the Leave War's sheets;
- **the medical move** — a row of its own: `pops.ts MEDMOVE`, drawn by `ui/MedMoveConfirm.tsx` (`if (!m) return null`),
  mounted at the top level;
- **the OIL question put before a change** — a row of its own: `state/view.ts OILWAIT`, drawn by `ui/OilWaitSheet.tsx
  OilWait` (`if (!w) return null`); `view.setPage` clears it on a page change;
- **the OIL question raised from a row of the Inputs list** — a row of its own, `oilrevise`. The row's "OIL" / "OIL?"
  button (`ui/InputsPage.tsx reviseOil`) raises `<OilConfirm>` from the page's own state (`oilConf`, l.281, l.1013)
  **with no Input window under it**, and its Save rewrites the row's OIL answer. Reported by
  `useGuardWork('oilrevise', !!oilConf)`. Of the sixteen uses of the five question components, in seven files, it is
  the only one found outside a counted window or a row of its own (the Leave War's three, in `BidPicker.tsx` and
  `SelectSheet.tsx`, were taken as inside their sheets, not traced); R3 counts each use (§3.4).
counts: **window**, all three. Shaded.

**(f) The Leave War — `leavewar/ui/Sheet.tsx`. One row, `open() = warWindowOpen()`, `where: 'leavewar'`.** Not shaded
(`modal={false}`): a person's window / My leave (`PersonWindow.tsx`), Grades (`GradesSheet.tsx`), Required figures
(`ReqPanel.tsx`), Selected days (`SelectSheet.tsx`). Shaded: Place a bid and the four award / posting sheets
(`BidPicker.tsx`), Settings, Open for bidding, Events and an event, the OIL tracker and its settings, a counter, a day's
list, Remarks, Rename, a manning row, the closed note, New leave war. counts: **window**. `Sheet.tsx OPEN` is kept by
mount, and the page stays mounted out of sight — §3.3. **(reading) — the page's E9b:** some of these sheets only show
things (a day's list, a counter, the OIL tracker, the closed note); he ruled the day panel alone (D2597). **Default:
each window he was never shown singly is checked one by one before the build; one with nothing to edit closes by
itself, the rest refuse.** The war's one row cannot tell its sheets apart, so it is shaped for either answer now
(Fable 1 of the third read): a sheet may be opened with `showOnly`, and `Sheet.tsx OPEN` keeps that mark and the
sheet's own closer beside its id; two rows read the one list — `lwsheet` (`window`: any sheet open without the mark)
and `lwshow` (`closes`: the marked ones, each closed by its own closer). **No sheet is marked until he has let E9b
stand and the builder's list of sheets, each with what it holds, has been put to him**; unmarked, every sheet refuses
as round 2 had it. The same check, and the same switch, for the Tracker's Show All and the board's two dialogs.
Not through `Sheet`, their state a component's own: the Legend and the under-manned list (`Chrome.tsx`, a scrim, a tap
outside closes), the flying working-out (`FlyRows.tsx .flywork`), an event's peek (`Matrix.tsx evPeek`), the bid
window's own pickers — counts: **no**, lists and peeks that a press outside closes (H2 names the Legend).

**(g) The Tracker — `tracker/app/core.js`, module state; everything is drawn inside `#page-tracker`. `where: 'tracker'`.**

| `TRK_WINDOWS` id | What | counts |

| `pop` (+ `popMsg`) | The marking window (`components/Pop.jsx`). It has no box to type in (pressed, P3 4), and **any press outside it closes it** — `tracker/App.jsx clickAway`, a `click` listener on the document that spares only `#pop` and `.ball`. So opening the Schedules list closes it before a row can be pressed, and a press on the badge would be refused and then close, in the same press, the window the note says to close. Left behind another tab on a desktop it stays set, and the pick then closes it without a word; on a phone it closes as he leaves the Tracker (pressed, P5 N1) | **E2 (N7) — default: `closes`, as today** (`close` through the bridge's closer, with `infoId` — for the one-press doors, which `clickAway` does not reach first); the one thing lost is a "Done on" day picked without a grade (the page's E2). Other answer: `window` while such a day waits — it cannot be built without changing `clickAway` (§8.3) |

Both shaded on a desktop (pressed, P1 4); neither is drawn on the phone's Tracker page as it opens | window; Show All: window **(reading)** — whether it only shows things was not put to him |

Whether a press outside closes each, as it closes `pop`, and whether each holds anything to edit: **not traced** — the builder reads each before its row is built (§14). **All four are drawn inside the Info panel, which a screen 1050 wide or less hides while its Flow chart tab shows (§3.1)** | **E4 (N12) — default: Down days and Failures (`downLog`, `failLog`) `closes`**, wherever they stand, hidden behind the Flow chart tab or in sight — they only show things. The two lull pop-ups: **pressed before the build**; one that can hold a choice not yet saved is `window`, and behind the Flow chart tab its note names the Info tab; one that cannot is `closes`. Other answer for Down days and Failures: `window`, the note naming the Info tab |

With nothing moved a pick closes it without a word; the view switch on his own squadron leaves it open (pressed, P1 4) | **no** — a mode; with edits unsaved the Tracker's question answers for it (asked at a pick and, D2627 A20, at the view switch); with nothing moved, at the view switch: **N10** |

**(h) Admin — a component's own state, reported by `useGuardWork` (§3.1). All `row` (D2595, widened by D2598).** Today
a pick asks nothing of any of them (pressed, P2 1): a changed Role is lost, the row open again on return; "Give
access" keeps its words across a pick and loses them at the view switch; "Add a person" **carries its typing to 149,
its Home squadron box now reading 149**; "+ New squadron" stays open on 149 with what was typed; a row with typing,
then the Squadrons section, then back: still open with its words — the sections are hidden, not removed, **so a row
counts while another Admin section shows, and its note says where** (D2627 A6: "…the open row on Users…").

Since D2599 / D2628 C11 it is a form opened by a button (§18.3): open with nothing in it — **no**; anything typed or changed (the callsign, the seat, the sign-in, the post-in date off today, the Home squadron off the page's own) — **row**; its Cancel clears and folds it, and then nothing counts | row, once something is in it (D2598); its note's words a draft `[WNS]` |
| A squadron's move (`MoveEditor`) · a place being edited (`PlaceEditor`) on Admin → Squadrons | `ui/SquadronsPanel.tsx` | row **(reading)** — D2598 names the row, Give access, "+ New squadron" and Add a person; these two opened rows are counted by the same rule, with the row's words |
| "Clear edit history…" armed | `ui/AdminPage.tsx ClearForm` | **no** — an inline two-tap control (press report A) |

**(i) Lists, menus and modes — in the table as `'no'` so the list is whole.** The bell's list (`pops.ts BELLLIST`), the
Schedules list (the component's own state in `ui/SquadronMenu.tsx` and `ui/Drawer.tsx DrawerSquadron`), the ☰ menu
(`DRAWER`), the Schedule Insights menu, the board's small menus (`.wavemenu`, `.stmenu`, `#sbMoreMenu`), a date box's
calendar (`ui/DateBox.tsx`), **the Medical page's "as of" calendar** (`ui/MedicalView.tsx calOpen`), the Add-people picker (`InputsCal.tsx .ic-pick`); **the Scheduler Board alone**
(`state/view.ts SBDAY` — not a window, D2627 A3: a line of another squadron in its own bell closes it and goes through,
as today; it lies over the whole screen, the top bar's picker, badge, ☰ and bell all under it, its only doors its own
bell and ✓ Done: pressed, P1 3), OIL Earn (`OILDAY` — it lives only on the board), an armed slot (`ARM`), an armed
"Discard N edits & load" or Withdraw (`RESTARM`, `UNPUBARM`), Logic's edit mode (`state/auth.ts LGEDIT`), Edit quals
(`ui/QualsPage.tsx`'s own `qEditing`), the "Set as default?" bar (`SECDEFOFFER`), the "Not swapped" bar (`OILBAR`), the
welcome-back card; and the one-shot hand-offs that are no window at all (`OILASK`, `OILOWN`, `INPEDIT_REFOUND`,
`AIRRID`, `BELLAT`, `INSIGHTS_ALL`, `OILSIGN_LATER`).
**One box inside a mode does count — Quals' "Add qualification"** (`#qNewQual`; the `qualsnew` report, `counts:
'row'`): words typed there are saved only by Add or Enter, the page stays mounted across a pick, and the mode's end
would otherwise either carry "LOW LEVEL", typed for 142, into 149's Quals or drop it unasked (Opus F-c; read on this
branch: `QualsPage.tsx` l.317, l.549, l.1189). **(reading):** it is typing in no window, which he put off with group
B's B12 (H13) — but Build 1 itself ends this mode (D2627 A4), so Build 1 must say what becomes of the box: it refuses
while the box holds words, under a lead of its own (§4.6), and with the box empty the mode ends as ruled. The other
answer: the mode ends and the words are cleared, nothing said. Put to him with §17's readings.
**Logic's and Quals' edit mode end at the press (D2627 A4) — part G, §18.1.** Pressed today (P1 5): an armed slot is
dropped by a pick; Logic's edit mode and Edit quals are not — 149's Logic and Quals open in their edit mode with "Done"
drawn, the change already typed saved into 142's record unasked, and Quals keeps "Done" in the member view (a fault,
fixed with it). Neither refuses: every change in an edit mode is saved as it is made.

**(j) A change not saved.** One row, `counts: 'unsaved'`, `open() = saveRefused()` (`storage/savewait.ts` — the saver's
state is `'failed'`, the band "Not saved — keep this page open" with Retry; it stays `'failed'` through a Retry in
flight, so a Retry-then-pick is refused without H8 — Fable's check). "Not kept" (`ui/savestatus-source.ts readBand() ===
'nokeep'`) is not a row: it never blocks (D2594).

**(k) Typing that is in no window** — a day's note, Help's bug box, a calendar's "+ Note", a Balances figure box, the
Logic search and Quals filter boxes: **not in Build 1** (H13 — group B, after Build 1: D2636); a `'no'` row each,
`why: 'H13'`. Today, pressed (P4 4): Help's words stay in the box across a pick, saved nowhere; a Balances figure and a
day's note are saved into 142's record at the pick.

### 3.3 A window he cannot see — answered (D2627 A7), and one case still open (N12)

**Its page is out of sight, and a tab leads back to it: it refuses, and the note says where** — "Blocked: close the
open window on the Leave War, then pick 149." / "…on the Tracker, then pick 149." `[ruled]` (D2627 A7). The Leave War
and the Tracker stay mounted when another tab is shown (`leavewar/LeaveWarPage.tsx`, `tracker/TrackerPage.tsx`), their
windows with them, so `warWindowOpen()` and the Tracker's states can be true with nothing of them on screen; letting
the pick through would leave that window behind a switch he made (what D2403 rules out), and closing it for him would
lose a bid half typed. Pressed today (P5 N1): My leave stays open behind the Inputs tab; a pick of 149 from there goes
through with nothing said, the window greyed when he opens the Leave War under 149. **Which Tracker windows refuse
this way** (A7's "stated in the build"): the rows of §3.2 (g) that count `window` — its questions and boxes, the ball
editor, Edit syllabus, Reorder, Copy, Flex settings; Show All as E9b's check decides; and of the four Info-panel
pop-ups those E4 leaves refusing — on its default none but a lull pop-up that can hold a choice. Edit chart layout is
a mode and the event's details close by themselves; the marking window is E2's. The Leave War's day panel closes by itself
wherever it is (D2597).

**Its page is one the squadron in view no longer offers: it does not count** (D2627 A7: "unless the squadron picked no
longer offers that page (it would be out of reach)"). Hunter has 149 picked by Plan, opens a person's window on the
Leave War, presses the Inputs tab; then a change he did not make leaves him a View of 149 only (a dated Plan ending at
midnight). 149 now offers View-only Sched and Help alone; the Leave War tab is not drawn (`perms.ts pageOffered`); the
sheet is still mounted, and its own Escape and Tab act only while the Leave War is the page showing (`Sheet.tsx`).
Counted, every pick, both view switches and every bell line would be refused with nothing on screen that can close
it. So the pick goes through, as today, and that window keeps today's handling for a change he did not make until
Build 2's card replaces it (D2405). The same for a Tracker window. Not pressed by anyone (§14).

**In the code:** `where` on each row and one function, `rowReachable(row)` — `where === 'any'`, or the page shown, or
a page `pageOffered(where)`; a row that is open and not reachable does not count. The note's "on the Leave War" / "on
the Tracker" is said exactly when the row's `where` is not the page shown.

**Still open — E4 (N12):** a Tracker window inside the Info panel while a narrow screen shows the Flow chart (§3.1's
table). The page is on screen and its Info tab is drawn, so he can reach the window in one press and nothing traps
him; but a note that says "…on the Tracker" names the page he is already looking at. **Default (the page's E4, which
the earlier revisions' "it refuses" gives way to): Down days and Failures close by themselves, as the show-only
windows he ruled; a lull pop-up that can hold a choice refuses, the note naming the Info tab (§8.3).** The `behind`
mark the Tracker's bridge hands over (§3.1) is then read only for a window that refuses; the panel is hidden by a
style rule, which jsdom does not load, so its proof is a browser
test (§4.7). **Nothing of E4 is built until he answers: it holds the four Info-panel rows, not step 1.**

### 3.4 The tests that fail when a window is added without a row

New file **`src/ui/msq-guard-rollcall.test.ts`** (reads source; no app is mounted). Each scan maps **every occurrence**,
not every file.

| Test | What it asserts | Fails first? |
|---|---|---|
| R1 "every flag has a row, and every row reads what its window is drawn from" | each `export let` of `ui/pops.ts` **and of `state/view.ts`** is the flag of a `GUARD_ROWS` row, or is named in the test's own census as not a window, with its reason (the shape of `pops.test.ts`'s check against `POPS_RESET`); each row's `open` is a function of `ui/windrawn.ts` (or of `ui/winorder.ts`, `state/typing-hold.ts`, `tracker/role.js`, `storage/savewait.ts`, or a `useGuardWork` or `useGuardCloses` report, or the Tracker’s bridge), and for each `windrawn` function **its component's file imports that name and calls it** | yes — no table yet |
| R2 "every movable window has a row" | every `<FloatWin id="…">` in a non-test file under `src/` is a key of `FLOAT_WHERE`, the map names no id the source lacks, **and `'avail'` and `'chg'` are not keys of it** | yes |
| R3 "every frame is counted, one by one" | in every non-test file under `src/`: each `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, `className="airpop…"`, `className="modal"`, `<Sheet ` — each call of `useSheetFocus`, `useEscClose`, `useEscWindow`, `escCloses`, `useFloatWin`, `useBarFree`, so a window drawn as a plain box is seen by its keys — **and each use of `<OilConfirm`, `<UpchitConfirm`, `<MedClashConfirm`, `<DocConfirm` and `<AskCard`** (sixteen today, in seven files) — is counted, and the test's census pins **the count per file**, each occurrence mapped to a row id, to the counted window it stands in, or to a `'no'` reason. A second dialog in `Modals.tsx` fails here with the words "give it a row in ui/guard.ts GUARD_ROWS" | yes |
| R4 "the Tracker's list is whole" | every module-level name `endSession()`'s body resets in `core.js` is a `TRK_WINDOWS` id or is in the test's own list of what is not a window (the modes, the search, the save words), with its reason; each dialog, `.modal` and `.pop` drawn in `tracker/components/*.jsx` is counted per file and mapped to an id; **each id drawn inside `SidePanel.jsx` carries `panel: 'info'`** | yes |
| R5 "the doors are these, and no others" | (a) the only non-test callers of `enterSquadron(` with a third argument, of `switchRoleInForce(`, `switchRoleView(`, `pickSquadron(` and `enterForLine(` are the ones in §4.1's table — the list as it stands at step 6; step 7 adds its new `enterForLine` callers in the commit that makes them; (b) **no non-test file but `state/store.ts` calls `view.setPage` or imports `setPage` from `state/view`** — every page change by a press goes through the page door (§4.5); (c) **every non-test call of the page door is in the test's census with its kind — `last`, `then`, `answer` or `asks first` (§4.5's table)**, read from the source: nothing but a redraw and the closing of its own menu follows a `last` call; an `asks first` function's first statement is its `ownPageRefused` line. A new call site fails until it is listed with a kind | (a) **a pin** — it passes the day it is written; (b) yes — six files reach `view.setPage` directly today; (c) yes |
| R6 "the checker fires" | the entry checker (below), fed a made-up squadron change by press: with a window open and no door's leave, it throws by name; with a leave and a window outside its `skip`, it throws; with the leave and only its skipped window, or with nothing open, it passes; a window opened in the turn of a refusal fails by name | yes |
| R7 "the scans catch a planted window" | each scan of R1–R5, run on a made-up source string holding one thing more than its census — a second dialog in a listed file, a new `export let` in `view.ts`, a new state reset in `endSession`, a second `<OilConfirm` in a page file, a statement after a `last` call of the page door — reports it | yes |
| R8 "a window closes by itself only on his ruling" | every row whose `counts` is `'closes'` carries a `close`, and no other row carries one; the test's own list of `'closes'` ids is exactly the six his rulings name — ALL AVAIL, Changes, Insights, `sansday`, `lwday`, `trkinfo` (D2596, D2597) — and **each further id joins only in the commit that cites his answer: `days` and the two settings rows (E1), `pop` (E2), `downLog`, `failLog` and a lull pop-up found to hold nothing (E4), `lwshow` and any window of E9b's list**. A row with `dirty` carries a `close` too and is in the same list. An id with no answer cited fails with "a window closes by itself only on his ruling" | yes |

**And one checker that runs under every existing test**, in the pattern of the order's §8 point 1 (the global set-up
`src/testing/marks-guard.ts` already installs one): **`state/guard.ts setEntryChecker`** — installed by the test set-ups
(`src/testing/` and `src/leavewar/test-setup.ts`, which import `ui/guard` too, so the guard answers in the headless
state tests of the census as well); a no-op in production. Two checks:

1. **No squadron or view change by press goes through with something open that its door did not leave out.** Each
   `refuse()` that passes hands the checker its door and `skip` — the leave, good for the synchronous turn it was
   given in. `enterSquadron` with `byPick` true and `switchRoleInForce` ask the checker first: it asks `refusalAt`
   again — **with the `skip` of the leave in hand, or with none where no door gave one** — and throws by name if that
   refuses. So the OIL list's card and the held window's chip pass with exactly their own window left out, a second
   window still fails them, a raw entry with anything open fails, and a raw entry with nothing open passes as today
   (`ui/memberview.test.tsx`, `leavewar/rolerestore.test.ts`). A press that waited is covered without more code: the
   Tracker's `again` re-runs the whole tap, and the landing asks the guard again with the door it kept (§4.5) — each
   ask is the leave for the entry that follows.
2. **Nothing opens behind a refusal.** At each `refuse()` that refuses, the checker notes which rows are open; at the
   end of that turn (a microtask) a counted row that is open and was not fails the test by name. The standing proof of
   §4.5's rule.

**Switched on in one step of its own, after the last door is guarded** (§10 step 6): `enterForLine` and the landing
both enter with `byPick` true, so any earlier and every existing bell test that taps across squadrons with something
open goes red before its door is built. Its allow-list — a test that drives a raw entry with something open, on
purpose: F4, the held chip's tests, step 3's kind (b) files — is in the checker's own file, by name with the reason,
and Build 2 empties it. **Its limit, said and not tested for:** it sees only an entry made with `byPick` true, and
the role switch. A squadron change his press causes through `maybeReenter` (`byPick` false — the Undo of N11 is the
one known) is invisible to it; R5 (a), §4.1's roll-call and the walk hold that family.

## 4. Part B — the doors refuse

### 4.1 Every way the squadron in view or the view changes by his own press

"The door" is the function the check is written in — never the control. No blank cells. **Four functions hold a check:
`pickSquadron`, `switchRoleView`, `enterForLine`, and the page door `store.ts setPage` (new — §4.5; with it
`ownSquadronFirst`, the same door for a press that opens a window and no page, and the landing's second ask after the
Tracker's question). And whatever a press would open after the page, the page door runs — never the caller (§4.5).**
Row 21, Undo's own, is E7's: built as step 6b on its default, left as today on its other answer.

| # | His press | Reaches | Refused by the guard? |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A row of the bar's Schedules list (`ui/SquadronMenu.tsx` `pick`) | `state/pick.ts pickSquadron` | **Refused** — in `pickSquadron` |
| 2 | A row of the ☰ menu's list (`ui/Drawer.tsx DrawerSquadron` `pick`) | `pickSquadron` | **Refused** — the same line; the menu then closes and is redrawn (D2627 A16). Today the menu stays open after a pick (pressed, P3 2) — and still does after a pick that goes through |
| 3 | The role badge, both ways (`ui/Shell.tsx #roleBadge`) | `state/store.ts switchRoleView` | **Refused** — in `switchRoleView` |
| 4 | The ☰ menu's "Switch to the member view" / "Back to the admin view" (`Drawer.tsx #drawerRole`) | `switchRoleView` | **Refused** — the same line. Its handler is `setDrawer(false); switchRoleView()` and only `switchRoleView`'s own `notify()` redraws: a refusal that returned before it left the menu on screen over the window — the handler now redraws itself (§4.4) |
| 5 | The Input window's own "Back to the admin view" chip (`InputWindow.tsx` `inped-rolechip`, drawn only on a window the member view holds) | `switchRoleView` | **Must not be** — it is the held window's own way back, and the only window open is the one it gives back. It passes `skip: ['inputedit', 'inputdlg']` (the Input window's two rows) and still asks every other row. No press reaches that state after Build 1 (pinned, B16); Build 2 removes the chip |
| 6 | A bell line of a squadron his own pages may be (`ui/topbits.tsx BellList` → `ui/topstate.ts openNotice`) | `state/belltap.ts enterForLine` → `enterSquadron` | **Refused** — in `enterForLine`; the line stays unread, the list open (§3 of the scenarios file). Today it enters, opens the window live and reads the line (pressed, P2 2) |
| 7 | **Any bell line that opens one of his own pages while a squadron he only views is picked** — a line of his own squadron (`enterForLine` answers `'here'`); a line of a squadron his pages may never be, which answers `'no'`: a third squadron he only views, one he has left, or the squadron in view itself while he is in the member view | `openNotice` → `openInputOnPage` / `openWarAt` / `openAdminUsers` → `setPage` → `store.ts landOnOfferedPage` → `landOnOwnPage` → `enterSquadron(home, …, true)` | **Refused** — at the page door, asked by `openNotice` before anything is marked read or closed, **and the window is run by the door, so none opens behind the refusal** (§4.5). The squadron comes back through `'no'` just as through `'here'` (§15, finding 1). Today: the bar goes to 142, the window opens (pressed, P2 5) |
| 8 | The bell itself, an access request (`topstate.ts bellTap` → `belltap.ts openBellRequest`) | `enterForLine`, then `openAdminUsers` → `setPage('admin')` | **Refused** — in `enterForLine` where the request's squadron would be entered, at the page door where Admin is not offered in the squadron in view; "…then press the bell again." `[ruled]` (D2627 A8), the bell staying lit. Today the tap enters 149 with a 142 Input window left greyed (pressed, P2 3) |
| 9 | The bell itself, his own unanswered OIL question on an input of another squadron (`bellTap`, the `oilHit` branch) | today: **no entry asked for** — `openInputOnPage` → `setPage('inputs')`. A Plan holder or master admin gets the input live under the squadron in view, and its answer saves into 142 under a bar reading 149 (security F2 — pressed, P6 A4). A View holder with a squadron he only views picked gets his own squadron back first, by the landing, nothing checked (pressed, P2 4) | **Refused** — in `enterForLine(inputSqn(…))`, the input's own squadron brought into view before its window (D2621 `[ruled]`; §5.3), and at the page door in the View holder's case — **the window, its toast and the OIL question with it: all three are the door's `then`** |
| 9b | **His own SANS reminder's line** — "Your SANS availability", a line his bell works out (`leavewar/sansunfiled.ts`, `k: 'sansdue'`), carrying no squadron. Hunter, SANS aircrew of 142 holding Plan of 149, 149 picked, taps it (Astra 4, Fable 2 of the third read; read on this branch) | today: `enterForLine` answers `'here'` for a line with no squadron; `openNotice` marks it read, sets the SANS tab and month, `askSansDay`, `setPage('inputs')` — and 149 offers Inputs, so the page door alone would pass it: **149's SANS calendar opens at his day**, where part G's "+ Commitment" now offers 149's SANS men and not him (§18.7). For one who only views the squadron picked, the landing brings his own back | **Refused as a line is, and his own squadron that day comes into view first:** the reminder's branch calls `enterForLine(homeOn(me(), today), again, 'line', o)` before anything is marked read, closed or set, then the page door. **(reading)** of D2621's "a bell line about such an input brings its squadron into view first" — the reminder is about no input; put to him with §17's list |

Refused for any other window or a change not saved — a state only a change he did not make can set up after Build 1; its note ends "…then try again." `[WNS]` (words3 W4) |

| 12 | "Check my quals" on the welcome-back card (`ui/WelcomeBack.tsx` — drawn over every page, `Shell.tsx`) | `setPage('quals')` → the landing | **Not a door after this build:** the card is drawn only while the squadron in view is his own (D2628 C4 — §18.5), where Quals is always offered. Nothing is built for the button itself (D2627 A18, Fable's change). Pressed today (P5 N3): the bar goes back to 142 and Quals opens, the card gone, nothing said; for a Plan holder it opens 149's Quals, where his row is not (P6 A9). The rule stands for any later button of the kind: the page door refuses it (belt) |
| 13 | Undo or Redo whose step lands on one of his own pages, a squadron he only views picked (`undo/timeline.ts` → `state/undo-wire.ts snapView`) | `setPage` → the landing | **No button reaches it today:** on a squadron he only views, the View-only Sched draws no Undo at all (pressed, P5 N3; P4 1d) — Ctrl+Z and the Help page were not pressed (§14). **The page door is its belt:** it stops the squadron changing whatever calls it. Should a road be found, the Undo itself must be refused before its step — `snapView` runs before the step's writes, so a refusal at the page alone would leave the step done and the note untrue: `globalUndo` would ask first, with the page the step would land on (`undo-wire.ts landingOf`). **The place:** `undo/timeline.ts globalUndo`'s own chain of refusals ahead of `snap(entry, 'undo')` — the line commented "refuse an unrestorable closure BEFORE the view-snap moves anything" — by a hook in the pattern of `hooks.restoreRefusal`; `globalRedo` has the twin chain. In `snapView` itself the page door's answer is tested — `land.then` runs only on `'went'` (§4.5). Falls away in Build 3 (D2593) |
| 14 | A page tab or a ☰ menu page (`Shell.tsx nav`, `Drawer.tsx go`) | `setPage` | **Never refuses** — a tab changes the page, never the squadron: while a squadron he only views is picked the tabs that would bring his own back are not drawn (`Shell.tsx hidden={viewOnly}`, `Drawer.tsx pageOffered`), so the page door is asked only for pages offered. Pinned (B17) |
| 15 | The Scheduler Board's own bell (`ui/SchedBoard.tsx #sbBell`) | rows 6–9, 11 | **Refused** as those rows — one `BellButton`, one `bellTap`. Today a 142 line pressed there closes the board and changes the squadron without a word (pressed, P1 3). The board alone is not a window (D2627 A3): with nothing else open the line goes through and the board closes, as today; its cancel-reason box and Sort all cover its bell |
| 16 | Logout (`ui/logout.ts logOut`) | `resetSession(null)` | **Must not be** — D2592: never refused. Unchanged in Build 1 |
| 17 | Signing in (`ui/Login.tsx` → `resetSession`, then `setPage(back)`) | `enterSquadron`, `byPick` false | **Must not be** — nothing can be open; not a press inside the app |

Pinned (§7, P5) |

A squadron change by his own press that is no pick: `maybeReenter` finds the pick stands on nothing (`perms.ts pickStandsOn`) and enters his home, `byPick` false. | `store.ts entryDue` → `enterSquadron` | **E7 — default: refused in Build 1 (step 6b)** while a window that counts is open, a row holds a change or a save has failed — and only then; with nothing open the Undo goes through as today. The rest of Undo waits with group B (D2636), and if he later takes B7 as recommended (the button greyed on 150, "Pick 142 to undo your changes there.") the greyed button replaces this refusal: **a piece built for the meantime**. **Its place:** the same chain of refusals in `globalUndo` as row 13, ahead of `snap(entry, 'undo')`, reading the entry's `inverse` for the squadron row (`UNIT_PFX`) of the squadron in view; the shape of that read is the builder's. The closed note's own "Open the Inputs page" in that state: §11 risk 20 |
